\f0\b\fs36 \cf0 \strokec2 4. Branch Scope and Production Context\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 The Acting branch covers 
\f0\b acting_scene
\f1\b0  and 
\f0\b monologue
\f1\b0  behaviour inside TapeCoach\'92s current shared six-field production architecture. It addresses spoken acting, monologue, scene/duologue, reader-mediated scenes, screen/self-tape tasks, stage/classical/contemporary text handling, speech delivery, self-tape assessability, professional presentation boundaries, no-brief claim restraint, accessibility-safe language, methodology neutrality, physical/fight/comedy limitations and Acting-specific output specificity.\
The revised branch outputs are intended to improve:\
\pard\tx220\tx720\pardeftab720\li720\fi-720\sa240\partightenfactor0
\ls1\ilvl0\cf0 \kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 Acting-form specificity.\
\ls1\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 Speech-delivery label containment for spoken-only Acting / Monologue.\
\ls1\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 Behavioural evidence anchoring in all substantive Acting feedback.\
\ls1\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 Screen/self-tape camera and eyeline specificity.\
\ls1\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 Separation of technical assessability from acting quality.\
\ls1\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 Separation of speech audibility from speech performance.\
\ls1\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 Live-room-only and process-only claim boundaries.\
\ls1\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 Reader-as-process support handling.\

\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 No-brief role/world invention protection.\

\cf0 Adversarial / non-regression tests:\
\pard\tx220\tx720\pardeftab720\li720\fi-720\sa240\partightenfactor0
\ls7\ilvl0\cf0 \kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 ACTING-OS-T01\'96T20 preserve ACTING-AUDIT-T01\'96T20.\
\ls7\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 Highest-priority: spoken-only no-singing label containment; monologue with no brief; reader-mediated scene; direct-to-camera versus off-camera task; poor lighting/framing; poor speech audibility; smartphone/home setup; polished but weak tape; response-to-direction overclaim; access-adapted context; speech difference; generic praise; invented role/world.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\ls8\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 Next-take specificity.\
\ls8\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 Reliability fallback.\
\ls8\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 No generic filler.\
\ls8\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 No production-polish reward.\
\ls8\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 No reader-quality reward/penalty.\
\ls8\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0

\pard\tx220\tx720\pardeftab720\li720\fi-720\sa240\partightenfactor0
\ls9\ilvl0\cf0 \kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 Generic phrases such as \'93grounded\'94, \'93natural\'94, \'93believable\'94, \'93connected\'94, \'93strong choices\'94, \'93clear relationship\'94, \'93good pacing\'94, \'93emotionally truthful\'94, \'93strong presence\'94, \'93good voice\'94, \'93professional self-tape\'94 and equivalents must be replaced with observable behavioural evidence.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Timestamp and evidence-density expectations:\
\pard\tx220\tx720\pardeftab720\li720\fi-720\sa240\partightenfactor0
\ls10\ilvl0\cf0 \kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 Acting reports should prioritise behavioural timestamps; for an assessable tape, notes should cover beginning/middle/end where useful and include at least one high-value strength moment and one improvement moment where justified.\
\ls10\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 The existing maximum of 8 timestamped notes remains unchanged.\

\f0\b\fs36 \cf0 \strokec2 10. Live Output QA / Bridge QA Status\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 True Acting-only live outputs were 
\f0\b not supplied
\f1\b0 . True Acting live-output QA has 
\f0\b not been completed
\f1\b0 .\
A Musical Theatre acting+song bridge QA was used as partial evidence for Acting-adjacent and shared-output risks. It can show whether MT acting+song component visibility and Acting/Vocal category visibility are preserved, and whether current production outputs still show generic wording, timestamp underproduction, false specificity, polish drift or acting-through-song weakness. It cannot prove Acting-only label containment, monologue-only behaviour, acting_scene-only behaviour, Acting-only comparison labels, speech-delivery category display or access-adapted Acting output behaviour.\
Current MT production examples showed:\
\pard\tx220\tx720\pardeftab720\li720\fi-720\sa240\partightenfactor0
\ls11\ilvl0\cf0 \kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 Acting and Song components displayed in MT reports.\
\ls11\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 Vocal remained visible where singing existed.\
\ls11\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 Generic phrases such as \'93grounded\'94, \'93naturalistic\'94, \'93emotionally connected\'94, \'93excellent vocal control\'94 and \'93professional\'94 appeared in some outputs.\
\ls11\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 Timestamp counts were sometimes too low for a 3\'965 minute multi-component tape.\
\ls11\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 Historical false specificity appeared around a claimed time-limit breach while the technical signal also said the length was within range.\
\ls11\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 professional_presentation language sometimes drifted into polish/resource wording such as \'93feels polished\'94 or \'93professional\'94, requiring stronger boundaries.\

\f0\b\fs36 \cf0 \strokec2 15. Compact Branch Handoff Pack\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 The Acting branch is mature enough for cross-branch consolidation and does not require an ACTING-PATCH. It is complete at research, synthesis, audit, revision, final-audit and output-specificity level, but its proposed refinements are not shipped production behaviour until later implementation and rollout.\
The branch source base contains 43 source rows across official admissions / conservatoire sources, formal acting frameworks, screen/self-tape guidance, professional and union guidance, accessibility/fairness sources and specialist form-gap sources. The strongest evidence supports acting-form specificity, the distinction between tape-observable and live-room/process evidence, speech delivery as Acting evidence rather than singing, screen/self-tape task-specific camera handling, self-tape assessability over production polish, reader-as-process support, no-brief grounding, accessibility-safe non-deficit handling and behavioural evidence anchors. Comedy, physical/fight evidence, methodology-neutral positive descriptors, neurodivergence-specific operational detail and positive speech descriptors under access conditions remain limitations rather than patch blockers.\
The audit produced ACTING-AUDIT-I01\'96I15 and ACTING-AUDIT-R01\'96R16. The revision implemented or conditionally contained these through ACTING-REV-C01\'96C16 and ACTING-REV-S01\'96S15. Final audit approved the revised baseline with explicit limitations under ACTING-FINAL-D01\'96D02. Output-specificity mapping then produced ACTING-OS-R01\'96R19, ACTING-OS-T01\'96T20 and ACTING-OS-I01\'96I10.\
Implementation-readiness implications are clear: future production Acting outputs must be form-specific, evidence-led, speech-label-safe, access-safe, anti-polish, no-brief-safe, methodology-neutral and timestamp-specific while preserving the current six-field architecture, Acting weights, server-side recomputation, caps, blockers, verdict thresholds, role-fit bounds, report schema, Step 1 / Step 2 pipeline and MT acting + song anchor.\
True Acting-only live-output QA has not been completed. The MT acting+song bridge QA provided useful partial evidence about component visibility, generic praise, timestamp underproduction, false specificity and presentation/polish drift, but it is not Acting release sign-off. Acting-only reports, comparison pages, JSON/report objects and exports are still required later to verify labels, renderer/export parity, no-brief handling, access-sensitive outputs and output enforcement.\
Stable ID families used: ACTING-S001\'96S043; ACTING-B1-F01\'96F10, ACTING-B2-F01\'96F09, ACTING-B3-F01\'96F08, ACTING-B4-F01\'96F10; ACTING-SYN-F01\'96F17; ACTING-AUDIT-I01\'96I15; ACTING-AUDIT-R01\'96R16; ACTING-REV-C01\'96C16; ACTING-REV-S01\'96S15; ACTING-FINAL-D01\'96D02; ACTING-OS-R01\'96R19; ACTING-OS-T01\'96T20; ACTING-OS-I01\'96I10.\
\pard\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
